# Changelog

All notable changes to this project are recorded here, generated from git history.
A release is a tag or a version bump; a group is the commit-subject prefix; a task
reference is the `[spec:<feature>/<task>]` a snapshot or fleet worker tagged the commit with.

## [v2.2.0] - 2026-10-24

### Fleet
- A qualified task off main keeps its wave owed; a qualified worker is not stalled ([79793f2](https://github.com/Sidiora-Labs/hyperfide/commit/79793f22cde027ae6e37b9c6339a7b9644dd1596), task hyperfide-v21/2.5)
- Sparse slice worktrees for local workers, hyperfide slice manifest, routed slice inspect ([202c360](https://github.com/Sidiora-Labs/hyperfide/commit/202c3602860fee3b142b9b96e7a5775c5b6f64ba), task hyperfide-v21/2.13)
- The sequential-wave gate re-asks git whether main is in the wave branch at the moment it decides — the freshness mark is taken at the top of the supervisor tick and the frontier is read later in it, so a land that reached main between the two left a mark that said fresh beside a spec that said landed, and 3.1 was cut from a wave branch without main and refused at its own done (32_supervisor, 1 release run in 3); a stale not-fresh only costs a tick; architecture doc carries the re-check ([c6a9ecc](https://github.com/Sidiora-Labs/hyperfide/commit/c6a9eccd6e0495303de9e441345eadd38b4c5681))
- One pull request body and one land log per branch — pr-<feature>-<wave>.md and land-<feature>-<wave>-<gate>.log for a wave, so two waves of one feature opening their pull requests at the same moment cannot hand each other their bodies, and a wave's land no longer overwrites the record of the wave landed before it ([31e4a11](https://github.com/Sidiora-Labs/hyperfide/commit/31e4a11a392f0b21b8dc8477b8c759ac8cd4bc09))
- A sequential wave's task waits until main is merged forward into its wave branch — a land merges into main before its gates and emits its event after them, so the main tree's spec said done before the land was known and the next wave's task could be cut from a stale wave branch and refused at done; 29_changelog follows the changelog model default ([3f34063](https://github.com/Sidiora-Labs/hyperfide/commit/3f3406368f635906b8947409620038ba065ebc91), task codify-v15/4.2)
- Waves side by side — [hierarchy] waves = parallel|sequential (parallel on git >= 2.38), cg spec start/fleet begin --ahead recording ahead on the lease, a strict done gate --force cannot pass in a fleet run, main merged forward into live wave branches after a land with conflicts escalated naming the projection finding, cg fleet sync, a projection build after land and sync; supervisor git status runs with --no-optional-locks so it no longer races a worker's index.lock ([2c7a914](https://github.com/Sidiora-Labs/hyperfide/commit/2c7a9141be1260b58eaf22df0bdb8de88fd63fb4), task codify-v15/4.2)
- A wave manager per wave and a task worker per task — wm-{feature}-{wave} on wave/{feature}/{wave} from main, w-{feature}-{task} on task/{feature}/{task} from its wave branch; [role.manager] aliases [role.feature]; templates without {wave}/{task} keep the older shape; plan and tree --json carry a manager per wave; begin cuts task/ from wave/, merge-up goes into the wave branch, land <wave|task-id> refuses naming the open tasks; the supervisor keeps a manager array per wave bounded by the manager max; workflow, help, docs/hierarchy.md and the VS Code fleet view follow ([72a72a3](https://github.com/Sidiora-Labs/hyperfide/commit/72a72a3be9be233d176c5ecee3b2d36f7e98616c), task codify-v15/4.1)
- Three-level concurrent tree — per-task branches, a merge lock instead of turn-taking, the main agent (opt-in), several features under one supervisor ([f7e1f48](https://github.com/Sidiora-Labs/hyperfide/commit/f7e1f48a393838342e19feee0a7ca3626b5ce1db), task codify-v11/4.1)
- Fleet_run_open and fleet_run_resume as planned ([2689ae8](https://github.com/Sidiora-Labs/hyperfide/commit/2689ae8ce36901fc55e7c6b8495587bab7921d89), task codify-v11/3.1)
- Durable supervisor — fleet up/down/pause/resume/runs, crash resume adopting live agents, opt-in approval gates ([88205b4](https://github.com/Sidiora-Labs/hyperfide/commit/88205b4879d948ebf30678fb24cae512760e60bc), task codify-v11/3.1)
- Per-role capabilities — driver, model, args, max, wall, spend, retries, stall, approve ([b66fe29](https://github.com/Sidiora-Labs/hyperfide/commit/b66fe29f6f6d2474f76478c7620fde7b16f9b266), task codify-v11/1.2)

- Merge task 4.2: waves side by side — [hierarchy] waves = parallel|sequential, cg spec start and cg fleet begin --ahead (--force no longer bypasses requires), the strict done gate, forward merges of main into live wave branches after a land (sup_forward_merge, fleet.forward), cg fleet sync (fleet.sync), a projection rebuild after every land and sync; the fleet-test flake fixed at its root: the supervisor's tree fingerprint and tree_clean run git --no-optional-locks status, so they no longer take index.lock under a worker's commit ([a6510c4](https://github.com/Sidiora-Labs/hyperfide/commit/a6510c4a4936f437ec6e3bc4b6707055927d3ae7), task codify-v15/4.2)

- Hook pre-bash: a shell grep, rg, ag, ack, find, fd, or ls -R over the tree is answered with its cg grep or cg files equivalent as PreToolUse additionalContext, once per session per command, never a permission decision, silent under CG_SHELL_ADVICE=0, [hooks] shell_advice = false, a path outside the tree, or a non-Bash payload; cg hook install registers it; the skill, agent context, MCP tool descriptions, and the start-work and orient prompts say to use grep_code, find_files, search_code, and get_context before any shell search ([d85ac3a](https://github.com/Sidiora-Labs/hyperfide/commit/d85ac3a6547bfe22101351d810a51761b8afc454), task codify-v15/1.4)

- Fleet discover: native sessions and subagents from the transcripts Claude Code and Codex already write, for hosts whose hooks never fired. Rows are created or completed column by column only where empty, so a hook's field is never overwritten; the discovered column records which columns came from transcripts. Worktrees named in meta.json are registered, pending spawns are claimed by toolUseId, and a transcript untouched for 15 minutes marks its agent stale. fleet status, fleet tree, serve's refresh and watch --fleet run discovery first under [fleet] discover (default true), once per 10 s per process, skipped by --no-discover. The transcript directory lookup is one helper, transcript_dirs, shared with recap. ### Fleet
- The sequential-wave gate re-asks git whether main is in the wave branch at the moment it decides — the freshness mark is taken at the top of the supervisor tick and the frontier is read later in it, so a land that reached main between the two left a mark that said fresh beside a spec that said landed, and 3.1 was cut from a wave branch without main and refused at its own done (32_supervisor, 1 release run in 3); a stale not-fresh only costs a tick; architecture doc carries the re-check ([e8fcc20](https://github.com/Sidiora-Labs/hyperfide/commit/e8fcc207ba1dd92a930ba892a451bd1dbf1fc804))
- One pull request body and one land log per branch — pr-<feature>-<wave>.md and land-<feature>-<wave>-<gate>.log for a wave, so two waves of one feature opening their pull requests at the same moment cannot hand each other their bodies, and a wave's land no longer overwrites the record of the wave landed before it ([5eeeb14](https://github.com/Sidiora-Labs/hyperfide/commit/5eeeb141d0216ad9e13aec4662d2b4b93e23eea2))
- A sequential wave's task waits until main is merged forward into its wave branch — a land merges into main before its gates and emits its event after them, so the main tree's spec said done before the land was known and the next wave's task could be cut from a stale wave branch and refused at done; 29_changelog follows the changelog model default ([4725578](https://github.com/Sidiora-Labs/hyperfide/commit/47255789433fe8a8532dea441feef373564dfa27), task codify-v15/4.2)
- Waves side by side — [hierarchy] waves = parallel|sequential (parallel on git >= 2.38), cg spec start/fleet begin --ahead recording ahead on the lease, a strict done gate --force cannot pass in a fleet run, main merged forward into live wave branches after a land with conflicts escalated naming the projection finding, cg fleet sync, a projection build after land and sync; supervisor git status runs with --no-optional-locks so it no longer races a worker's index.lock ([2f1adba](https://github.com/Sidiora-Labs/hyperfide/commit/2f1adba6193b782918d9c568fd383c0ed5192a0c), task codify-v15/4.2)
- A wave manager per wave and a task worker per task — wm-{feature}-{wave} on wave/{feature}/{wave} from main, w-{feature}-{task} on task/{feature}/{task} from its wave branch; [role.manager] aliases [role.feature]; templates without {wave}/{task} keep the older shape; plan and tree --json carry a manager per wave; begin cuts task/ from wave/, merge-up goes into the wave branch, land <wave|task-id> refuses naming the open tasks; the supervisor keeps a manager array per wave bounded by the manager max; workflow, help, docs/hierarchy.md and the VS Code fleet view follow ([88b92b7](https://github.com/Sidiora-Labs/hyperfide/commit/88b92b743e5bc8d9490773a57cb1f458d0678a02), task codify-v15/4.1)
- Three-level concurrent tree — per-task branches, a merge lock instead of turn-taking, the main agent (opt-in), several features under one supervisor ([5f49b41](https://github.com/Sidiora-Labs/hyperfide/commit/5f49b4172517b54cd816d0ba100ca6724eca4d24), task codify-v11/4.1)
- Fleet_run_open and fleet_run_resume as planned ([6c8bc03](https://github.com/Sidiora-Labs/hyperfide/commit/6c8bc03afb4822de15a9cf04cfd2500c46d48be3), task codify-v11/3.1)
- Durable supervisor — fleet up/down/pause/resume/runs, crash resume adopting live agents, opt-in approval gates ([e638d7f](https://github.com/Sidiora-Labs/hyperfide/commit/e638d7f8f1232357d2278c94f99c0f930c549b04), task codify-v11/3.1)
- Per-role capabilities — driver, model, args, max, wall, spend, retries, stall, approve ([ade87e1](https://github.com/Sidiora-Labs/hyperfide/commit/ade87e1dc533b0affac8b7304f8b37153de35e1d), task codify-v11/1.2)
- Branch lifecycle — cg fleet begin/merge-up/land/pr/checkpoint, schema v16 (attempts branch/worktree/parent, memories branch/class/confidence), spec_claim shared with the fleet, git_run/worktree helpers, fake gh fixture ([83d3e2e](https://github.com/Sidiora-Labs/hyperfide/commit/83d3e2ed4608390063326778654bc0bf084c43da), task codify-v10/2.2)

- Symbol, interface, near, and textual findings from every build (src/conflicts.c), kept in projection_findings with projection.conflict once, projection.resolved when a fix lands, and projection.built per build; cg projection findings lists the live ones with ages; the agents on the branches involved are steered once per finding unless [projection] steer = false; 52_conflicts.sh ([0074aef](https://github.com/Sidiora-Labs/hyperfide/commit/0074aef29021a347a80b2ba5738c9132c45211f3), task codify-v15/3.2)
- Cg projection build folds every active branch onto the base with merge-tree, uncommitted work through shadow commits from a copied index, conflicts folded with markers and attributed to the earlier branches, written to refs/codify/projection and the projections table; fingerprint no-op, --force, --strict, --branch, the build lock with --wait, status, diff, show, and --index checking the projection out under .codegraph/worktrees/projection indexed as branch codify/projection; [projection] base, stale, exclude, index in codify.kvx; schema 18 ([6435620](https://github.com/Sidiora-Labs/hyperfide/commit/64356205512df955106ce4dd8c596a7ad122b524), task codify-v15/3.1)

- Merge task 3.3: the projection follows the work — cg hook post-edit and the work events of cg hook event mark the projection dirty and start one detached cg projection build --quiet under the projection lock, at most once per [projection] min_interval (15s) unless [projection] auto = false; cg watch and watch --fleet build when edits settle, cg serve and the fleet supervisor after merge, land, begin and spawn events; fleet merge-up and land print the branch's known findings before merging; projection status groups the live findings by branch with ages, brief and state name the first two, fleet tree --json carries projection{}, the projection_status MCP tool, a Projection node in the editor's fleet view; a background build drops the triggering agent's identity so its events are nobody's work; 53_projection_live.sh and a 14_vscode fleet section ([4097ab8](https://github.com/Sidiora-Labs/hyperfide/commit/4097ab8a4ee72e4a7162b65c8cc1afa7f7982760), task codify-v15/3.3)

- Merge task 4.2: waves side by side — [hierarchy] waves = parallel|sequential, cg spec start and cg fleet begin --ahead (--force no longer bypasses requires), the strict done gate, forward merges of main into live wave branches after a land (sup_forward_merge, fleet.forward), cg fleet sync (fleet.sync), a projection rebuild after every land and sync; the fleet-test flake fixed at its root: the supervisor's tree fingerprint and tree_clean run git --no-optional-locks status, so they no longer take index.lock under a worker's commit ([0392b62](https://github.com/Sidiora-Labs/hyperfide/commit/0392b6218ccd349927dfaa46b130d1d62eee62e5), task codify-v15/4.2)
- Projection follows the work: cg hook post-edit and work events from cg hook event mark the projection dirty and start one detached cg projection build --quiet under the projection lock, once per [projection] min_interval (15s) unless [projection] auto = false; cg watch and watch --fleet build when edits settle, cg serve and the fleet supervisor after merge, land, begin and spawn events; fleet merge-up and land print the branch's known findings before merging; projection status groups the live findings by branch with ages, brief and state name the first two, fleet tree --json carries projection{} with per-branch counts, the projection_status MCP tool, and the editor fleet view gains a Projection node whose branch rows open the file in that worktree and badges on involved branches; 53_projection_live.sh and a 14_vscode fleet section ([38c7eb8](https://github.com/Sidiora-Labs/hyperfide/commit/38c7eb8821cd9c8d39e93d8da6ec8fa03a0d1d39), task codify-v15/3.3)
- Merge task 1.3: the index at scale — parallel walk (src/walk.c), git-assisted change detection under [index] walk, a contentless body index on SQLite 3.43+, files.base with idx_file_base and idx_import_target, one FTS purge per chunk (body_tri included), phase timings and peak RSS in sync --json, the scale fixture, 46_scale.sh and scripts/bench.sh; body_fts follows its own DDL when it already exists ([ea31d22](https://github.com/Sidiora-Labs/hyperfide/commit/ea31d22190e2c43d5630694b92e74761fafe449d), task codify-v15/1.3)
- Merge task 1.4: cg hook pre-bash — shell grep, find, rg, fd and ls -R advice toward cg grep and cg files, once per command per session, under [hooks] shell_advice; the skill, agent context, MCP descriptions and prompts say the same ([c70bd74](https://github.com/Sidiora-Labs/hyperfide/commit/c70bd7453b1e7d382e593ba8f43f76c3c35ae0bb), task codify-v15/1.4)
- Merge task 3.2: projection conflicts — symbol, interface, near, content, delete and add-add findings over the fold, stored in projection_findings, projection.conflict and projection.resolved events, cg projection findings, and one steer per agent per new finding under [projection] steer; an unknown hook subcommand drains only the stdin that arrives ([b1350c6](https://github.com/Sidiora-Labs/hyperfide/commit/b1350c630bd185f1fd995937ee62a4744787a91f), task codify-v15/3.2)

- Hook pre-bash: a shell grep, rg, ag, ack, find, fd, or ls -R over the tree is answered with its cg grep or cg files equivalent as PreToolUse additionalContext, once per session per command, never a permission decision, silent under CG_SHELL_ADVICE=0, [hooks] shell_advice = false, a path outside the tree, or a non-Bash payload; cg hook install registers it; the skill, agent context, MCP tool descriptions, and the start-work and orient prompts say to use grep_code, find_files, search_code, and get_context before any shell search ([08fa148](https://github.com/Sidiora-Labs/hyperfide/commit/08fa148d5f9a43bb32128a588fabc7feaaf6646f), task codify-v15/1.4)

- Fleet discover: native sessions and subagents from the transcripts Claude Code and Codex already write, for hosts whose hooks never fired. Rows are created or completed column by column only where empty, so a hook's field is never overwritten; the discovered column records which columns came from transcripts. Worktrees named in meta.json are registered, pending spawns are claimed by toolUseId, and a transcript untouched for 15 minutes marks its agent stale. fleet status, fleet tree, serve's refresh and watch --fleet run discovery first under [fleet] discover (default true), once per 10 s per process, skipped by --no-discover. The transcript directory lookup is one helper, transcript_dirs, shared with recap. ([96497d3](https://github.com/Sidiora-Labs/hyperfide/commit/96497d36078b8ee12219bdf18ca0742c5e2bfb04), task codify-v15/2.4)

### Fleet
- The sequential-wave gate re-asks git whether main is in the wave branch at the moment it decides — the freshness mark is taken at the top of the supervisor tick and the frontier is read later in it, so a land that reached main between the two left a mark that said fresh beside a spec that said landed, and 3.1 was cut from a wave branch without main and refused at its own done (32_supervisor, 1 release run in 3); a stale not-fresh only costs a tick; architecture doc carries the re-check ([c6a9ecc](https://github.com/Sidiora-Labs/hyperfide/commit/c6a9eccd6e0495303de9e441345eadd38b4c5681))
- One pull request body and one land log per branch — pr-<feature>-<wave>.md and land-<feature>-<wave>-<gate>.log for a wave, so two waves of one feature opening their pull requests at the same moment cannot hand each other their bodies, and a wave's land no longer overwrites the record of the wave landed before it ([31e4a11](https://github.com/Sidiora-Labs/hyperfide/commit/31e4a11a392f0b21b8dc8477b8c759ac8cd4bc09))
- A sequential wave's task waits until main is merged forward into its wave branch — a land merges into main before its gates and emits its event after them, so the main tree's spec said done before the land was known and the next wave's task could be cut from a stale wave branch and refused at done; 29_changelog follows the changelog model default ([3f34063](https://github.com/Sidiora-Labs/hyperfide/commit/3f3406368f635906b8947409620038ba065ebc91), task codify-v15/4.2)

### Fleet
- Waves side by side — [hierarchy] waves = parallel|sequential (parallel on git >= 2.38), cg spec start/fleet begin --ahead recording ahead on the lease, a strict done gate --force cannot pass in a fleet run, main merged forward into live wave branches after a land with conflicts escalated naming the projection finding, cg fleet sync, a projection build after land and sync; supervisor git status runs with --no-optional-locks so it no longer races a worker's index.lock ([2c7a914](https://github.com/Sidiora-Labs/hyperfide/commit/2c7a9141be1260b58eaf22df0bdb8de88fd63fb4), task codify-v15/4.2)
- A wave manager per wave and a task worker per task — wm-{feature}-{wave} on wave/{feature}/{wave} from main, w-{feature}-{task} on task/{feature}/{task} from its wave branch; [role.manager] aliases [role.feature]; templates without {wave}/{task} keep the older shape; plan and tree --json carry a manager per wave; begin cuts task/ from wave/, merge-up goes into the wave branch, land <wave|task-id> refuses naming the open tasks; the supervisor keeps a manager array per wave bounded by the manager max; workflow, help, docs/hierarchy.md and the VS Code fleet view follow ([72a72a3](https://github.com/Sidiora-Labs/hyperfide/commit/72a72a3be9be233d176c5ecee3b2d36f7e98616c), task codify-v15/4.1)

### Db
- A files table stamped v18 before files.base gains the column on open, filled from path ([80dad18](https://github.com/Sidiora-Labs/hyperfide/commit/80dad1832249a6b5bafc563ad132acb48392da58), task codify-v15/1.3)

### Editor
- Native sessions and their subagents under a Sessions root in the fleet view, patched live from agent.native.* through the serve reducer, with commands to open a native agent's worktree and transcript; cg serve snapshot carries the native rows after a weak discovery hook task 2.4 replaces ([83ae72a](https://github.com/Sidiora-Labs/hyperfide/commit/83ae72a6b637eb3a1c5bd771d3feaac84c1afbf6), task codify-v15/2.3)

### Files
- Cg files answers globs, path words and directories from the files table and a trigram file_fts (schema 18); --dirs maps the tree with counts and dominant language; --path, --lang and --no-tests narrow search, context and survey inside the SQL; a large directory's context outline leads with its subdirectories; find_files over MCP and the filters on search_code and get_context ([be9676e](https://github.com/Sidiora-Labs/hyperfide/commit/be9676e15ea6e73de156764feae5927cf8dc52c8), task codify-v15/1.2)

### Grep
- A body_tri short of the files that carry a body makes grep scan the whole scope and say so (full scan — body_tri incomplete, full_scan:"body_tri incomplete" in --json) instead of missing files silently; the next whole index pass re-inserts the missing rows and reports N trigram rows repaired; --path, --lang and --no-tests go through the shared QueryFilter so grep narrows exactly as search, context, survey and files do ([a9c7d15](https://github.com/Sidiora-Labs/hyperfide/commit/a9c7d1543a66819914622d83ab885949a64c1bd3), task codify-v15/1.5)
- Cg grep searches lines through a trigram index (body_tri, contentless on SQLite 3.43+), scans candidates with literal or POSIX regex matching across worker threads, places each hit in its enclosing symbol, ranks and budgets the output, supports -C, --files, --count, --symbol, --kind, --path, --lang and --json, and answers grep_code over MCP; schema 18 ([5c85f3b](https://github.com/Sidiora-Labs/hyperfide/commit/5c85f3bbcacc36ad4192b0f25e2c8c5b7c2e9174), task codify-v15/1.1)

### Index
- The index at scale — parallel walk, git-assisted candidates ([index] walk), contentless body_fts on SQLite >= 3.43 (fts_mode, cg info), files.base lookups replace LIKE '%/name', one FTS purge per chunk, walk/phase_ms/peak_rss_kb in --json; schema 18; tests/scale generator, 46_scale, scripts/bench.sh, measured table in docs/sync.md ([98036a6](https://github.com/Sidiora-Labs/hyperfide/commit/98036a6e31f711b18f205acd88af4163d2f3547c), task codify-v15/1.3)

### Integrate
- Cg integrate apply renders the Claude Code plugin marketplace under ~/.codify/plugins/claude-code (manifests, hooks.json calling cg by absolute path, .mcp.json, the workflow skill, brief/fleet/projection commands) with version-stamped codify-owned markers, installs it through the claude CLI or prints the two commands, and writes .codex/hooks.json; --no-plugin skips both; detect and doctor report the plugin's install and version state and the Codex /hooks trust step; cg hook install leaves settings.json to an installed plugin; every hook exits 0 outside a project or for an unknown subcommand; docs/integrate.md; 48_plugin.sh ([3ef7dbb](https://github.com/Sidiora-Labs/hyperfide/commit/3ef7dbba2e09d4d916547b772ec6e09df904c1b3), task codify-v15/2.1)

### Plugin
- No WorktreeCreate or WorktreeRemove hooks — a WorktreeCreate hook replaces git's worktree creation and a silent one makes it fail; hooks.json keeps only the documented hooks key with the ownership marker riding in each command; spec, test and doc follow ([427b460](https://github.com/Sidiora-Labs/hyperfide/commit/427b460f4529f34ed5dcf6d2a9ec2e2a0a700a2e))

### Projection
- A background build carries no agent, run or task identity from whoever triggered it, so its events are nobody's work ([2a5c7a8](https://github.com/Sidiora-Labs/hyperfide/commit/2a5c7a82b4dab15badb9be39f29aaa7309976aae), task codify-v15/3.3)
- Symbol, interface, near, and textual findings from every build (src/conflicts.c), kept in projection_findings with projection.conflict once, projection.resolved when a fix lands, and projection.built per build; cg projection findings lists the live ones with ages; the agents on the branches involved are steered once per finding unless [projection] steer = false; 52_conflicts.sh ([add2646](https://github.com/Sidiora-Labs/hyperfide/commit/add26460d1d9567028b64f2a0c1dee840e9c988d), task codify-v15/3.2)
- Cg projection build folds every active branch onto the base with merge-tree, uncommitted work through shadow commits from a copied index, conflicts folded with markers and attributed to the earlier branches, written to refs/codify/projection and the projections table; fingerprint no-op, --force, --strict, --branch, the build lock with --wait, status, diff, show, and --index checking the projection out under .codegraph/worktrees/projection indexed as branch codify/projection; [projection] base, stale, exclude, index in codify.kvx; schema 18 ([f8e7555](https://github.com/Sidiora-Labs/hyperfide/commit/f8e7555c08e5fee6254d0be553cf7b5cbda907da), task codify-v15/3.1)

- Fleet discover: native sessions and subagents from the transcripts Claude Code and Codex already write, for hosts whose hooks never fired. Rows are created or completed column by column only where empty, so a hook's field is never overwritten; the discovered column records which columns came from transcripts. Worktrees named in meta.json are registered, pending spawns are claimed by toolUseId, and a transcript untouched for 15 minutes marks its agent stale. fleet status, fleet tree, serve's refresh and watch --fleet run discovery first under [fleet] discover (default true), once per 10 s per process, skipped by --no-discover. The transcript directory lookup is one helper, transcript_dirs, shared with recap. ([e0d873d](https://github.com/Sidiora-Labs/hyperfide/commit/e0d873ded75c877c7af19dda73f4bd44cf8ce1eb), task codify-v15/2.4)

## [1.1.0] - 2026-09-28

### Highlights

Version 1.1.0 is a small release. `.env` has been added to `.gitignore`, so Git ignores `.env` files. The changelog generation message has been updated for clarity, and the documentation on the changelog and contribution processes has been enhanced.

### Changelog
- Add .env to .gitignore; update changelog generation message for clarity; enhance documentation on changelog and contribution processes ([295dd88](https://github.com/Sidiora-Labs/hyperfide/commit/295dd8831571261813252ca7522916dd728f041e))

## [0.1.0] - 2026-08-13

### Highlights

This is the first release of Codify, a single-binary developer tool. The 0.1.0 commit list contains only a single "First commit" entry, so there are no individual changes to call out. Codify's code graph, spec-driven task workflow, and agent fleet are the intended scope of the project, but this release does not document specific commands or behaviour beyond that starting point.
